### Independent verifier

The draft is careful and mostly accurate. Every cited literature result I could open exists essentially as stated, and the central structural identification is correct. These include Lagarias 1999, Hinkkanen 1997, Sondow-Dumitrescu, MSZ, Audenaert 2011, Sra 2026, Polson v9, Zhang, GORZ, CNV, Dimitrov-Lucas, Farmer 2022, Ki 2006, Ki-Kim 2000, Kim 1996, Ki-Kim-Lee, Lagarias 2005, Conrey-Li, Sekatskii, Hansen-Ji-Tomiyama, Tyrtyshnikov/Beckermann and Pemantle-Subramanian.  The central identification is that the repo's real-node safe-axis matrix H_ij=(F(x_i)+F(x_j))/(x_i+x_j) is exactly Audenaert's anti-Loewner (Kwong) matrix of F. So the all-order criterion L-91904 is Audenaert eq. (2) plus analytic continuation, and is equivalent to Sra Prop A.1 / Polson Thm 26 / Zhang Lemma 3. I re-derived the K+L and K-L identities and the Laplace-transform implication from Sra's H>0, H'<0 to order-2 positivity and tp-concavity. I also checked the repo's order-3 factorization exactly.  Main problems:  (1) Relation labels on bundled statements are too strong. In PA1, 'identical' holds for the scalar Re F>0 interface only; the Pick-matrix step is the standard Caratheodory-Pick consequence. In PA4, 'repo_is_special_case' covers only the all-order criterion, not the order-3 factorization or the Loewner-Hankel congruence. In PA10, the repo's alpha_5>0.98 is by its own account weaker than Conrey's published 0.9970, so it is not a partial overlap.  (2) PA11 understates Ki-Kim 2000 and misses the classical Bezoutian inertia. Ki-Kim proved Fourier-Polya for every genus-0 real entire function without any strip hypothesis. That applies directly to h(s)=xi(1/2+sqrt s)/c0, though not to Xi of genus 1 or its z-derivatives. The repo's corrected Bezoutian inertia formula is the classical Hermite/Krein-Naimark theorem, which the draft does not mention.  (3) PA7 asserts 'B in CBF <=> p Stieltjes' as if it were abstract. Only the reverse implication holds in general; the chain is valid here only because each side is separately RH-equivalent.  (4) PA14 misreads the repo. The repo does not say a real-center generalized Li theorem is missing. Sekatskii is exactly the v=0 specialization of the repo's Mobius map and does not touch the refuted complex-center object.  (5) The novelty assessment says the critical-reserve 'mechanism is the same' as Sra Lemma 2.2. Only the tail bound 2(log U+1)/U is identical. Sra controls oscillation delay in the Laplace variable; the repo allocates curvature shares in s. Priority holds against the cited Sra and Polson versions, but earlier Polson versions (Apr and Jul 2026) predate the repo integration and were not checked.  (6) Citation details. Kim PAMS 124 is 819-830. The English Sekatskii is Ukrainian Math. J. 66 (2014) 415-431. SSV Stieltjes functions are Ch. 2. Hansen-Ji-Tomiyama is for intervals other than R. In OB3, Pemantle-Subramanian and Campbell-O'Rourke-Renfrew show universality only within real-rooted classes, not RH-blindness. In OB4, use KKL's normalization (Lambda=4 lambda^(0)), and treat the 'zero-margin' reading as heuristic.  (7) PA15 recommends importing Krein-Langer, but the repo already has a Krein-Langer lemma (safe_line L-91034). It should be a cross-reference. The 'uniqueness set' remark needs the sesqui-analytic Taylor-coefficient argument (or Audenaert), not the open-subset lemma.  Several evidence levels can be upgraded: Lagarias 1999, KKL and OB6 via zbMATH reviews. I found no error in any repository mathematical statement examined, and the bridge's own derived claims (order 2 and tp-concavity from H>0 and H'<0; reciprocal concavity not implied) check out.

**Missed prior art flagged:**
- M. G. Krein, M. A. Naimark, The method of symmetric and Hermitian forms in the theory of the separation of the roots of algebraic equations (Kharkov 1936; Engl. transl. Linear Multilinear Algebra 10 (1981) 265-308): the Hermite/Sylvester inertia theorem for Bezoutians and Hankel forms of rational functions. Each real pole contributes the sign of its residue and each conjugate pole pair contributes one negative and one positive square. This is exactly the corrected F01 formula n_-(B_p)=#{real c: rho_c>0}+#{c in C+: p'(c)=0}.
- H. Ki, Y.-O. Kim, Duke Math. J. 104 (2000) 45-73, Thm 4.1, used more strongly than in the draft: Fourier-Polya is proved for every real entire function of genus 0, so it applies unconditionally to h(s)=xi(1/2+sqrt s)/c0 (order 1/2). This gives RH iff h and all its s-derivatives have no Fourier critical points. It is relevant to TT6, F01 and OPEN_CUTS section 4.
- R. Garunkstis, On a positivity property of the Riemann xi-function, Lith. Math. J. 42 (2002) 140-145 (Zbl 1026.11070): inf_t Re(xi'/xi)(sigma+it)=(xi'/xi)(sigma) on every zero-free half-plane sigma>a, including sigma>=1. This is the precise extension of Lagarias's sigma>10 result named in PA1.
- J. C. Lagarias, Acta Arith. 89 (1999) Thms 1.2-1.3 as distinct results: the inf-at-t=0 property is unconditional only for sigma>=1+9n_K^{-1/3} (sigma>=10 for zeta) and is RH-conditional for all sigma>1/2.
- R. Bhatia, T. Jain, Inertia of Kwong matrices, arXiv:2307.16007 (2023), and M. K. Kwong, On the definiteness of the solution of a certain matrix equation, Linear Algebra Appl. 108 (1988) 177-198: origin of the anti-Loewner/Kwong matrices and their inertia. Also R. Bhatia, Positive Definite Matrices (Princeton 2007) p. 195, where the characterization question is posed.
- M. W. Coffey, Asymptotic estimation of xi^(2n)(1/2): on a conjecture of Farmer and Rhoades, Math. Comp. 78 (2009) 1147-1154. Farmer 2022 notes Ki's cosine limit also follows from Coffey's proposition, so it should be checked before claiming novelty for the sqrt(m/log m) window.
- P. Freitas, A Li-type criterion for zero-free half-planes of Riemann's zeta function, J. London Math. Soc. 73 (2006) 399-414 (Zbl 1102.11046); F. C. S. Brown, Li's criterion and zero-free regions of L-functions, J. Number Theory 111 (2005). Both concern shifted-center and half-plane Li-type criteria and bear on proof-boundary-corrections section 4 and TT7.
- G. Csordas, R. S. Varga, Necessary and sufficient conditions and the Riemann hypothesis, Adv. Appl. Math. 11 (1990) 328-357 (Thm 2.9), together with J. L. W. V. Jensen, Acta Math. 36 (1913) 181-195, and Polya 1927 condition I'. These Jensen-type convexity/Laguerre criteria should be checked against safe_line OPEN.OPERATOR.RADIAL_CURVATURE (T-91002). They are cited only in a transplantable tool, not in prior art.
- B. Roynette, M. Yor, Ensembles de fonctions zeta... / Theoreme V.3.2 (2005): Lambda in PF_inf iff RH, and the reciprocal-xi law under RH. This is the earliest source for the PF_inf form in PA7, before Grochenig 2020.
- H. L. Montgomery, R. C. Vaughan, Multiplicative Number Theory I (2007), Exercise 13.2.1(e): horizontal monotonicity of \|xi\| under RH, which predates Sondow-Dumitrescu (PA3).
